I have this laptop — Lenovo LOQ i7-13650HX, 24GB RAM, 512GB SSD, RTX 4050.
It’s powerful for gaming, multitasking, smooth 144Hz display, and upgrade-friendly.

Serious Disadvantages of Lenovo LOQ i7-13650HX

1. Runs extremely hot under heavy load.


2. Cooling fans get very loud during gaming or multitasking.


3. Battery drains quickly, often lasting only 2–3 hours.


4. Heavy weight (2.38kg) makes it less portable.


5. Display brightness is limited (300 nits), poor for outdoor use.


6. Ships with DOS version, requiring separate OS installation.


7. Performance throttles when overheating.


8. Speakers are weak with low volume and poor sound quality.


9. Limited storage (512GB) fills up quickly with games and files.


10. No SD card reader for quick media transfer.


11. Limited USB-C connectivity and no Thunderbolt support.


12. Webcam is only 720p, below modern standards.


13. No fingerprint sensor or biometric login options.


14. Build is mostly plastic, less durable and premium.


15. Screen has glare issues in bright environments.


16. Large and heavy charger is inconvenient to carry.


17. BIOS and software updates have been reported unstable.


18. Resale value is significantly lower compared to Lenovo Legion series.


19. Dust accumulation is high, and vents are difficult to clean.

Scrambler is a good bike, 80-90kmph is a breeze for this bike. Although u can feel a little bit of buzz on the handle bars. 100-110kmph should be managable. Although it can cross 140kmph as well. 
Just buy a few accessories like usd fork seal cover, instrument cluster protector and a monoshock cover. 
The other thing i recommemd is changing the knucle guards, those stock ones are weak and useless. Gravel haru ma u may come out with just a broken knickle guard but tarmac ma u could even lose a portion of ur brake lever. 
Also think about investing in auxiliary lights, the stock ones aren't enough. Rati saaro parcha

LFP is much cheaper chemistry than NMC, and thus for any expensive car, LFP is not used. That is why we see LFP in cheaper cars and NMC in expensive cars. I hope the video corrects this. If LFP was used, the weight of this car would have increased excessively and range reduced, which is why the expensive NMC chemistry is used here. 

However, for vehicles produced after May 2024, we will be able to see high quality LFP chemistry (Shenxing 4C Plus) from CATL which is a big breakthrough in LFP chemistry allowing it to almost match the quality of these NMC batteries in terms of charging speed and energy density.

Thanks
It's definitely a very good bike with practical approach at best. But I have to say it's an incomplete offering from the Bazaz !
Few obvious caveats I've found like;
1. Absence of sixth gear
- I know you can live without a sixth gear due to having tall gearing ratio & sheer power & torque figure particularly in this bike, But the advantages you will get from having a sixth gear surpass this very notion. 
Having a sixth gear means the engine of your bike will face less pressure during highway cruise. & This also automatically means lesser vibration & better fuel economy.  
2. Narrow-thin tyres 🙄/no radial
- RTR bikes are notorious for their polio infected tyres 🙄 & unfortunately it seems to be the similar case.
 Rear tyre at least should be 140/70 section & radial. 130 is too narrow for its segment and price. Fz25 & Gixxer are offering wider tyres with radial with dual channel all at the same time, Indians should put safety at the highest priority in their product making. I can say just by looking at those polio tyres they're not good for cornering, during rainy days & high speed cruising.
3. Poor Fuel economy
- I have seen many YouTube videos touting mileage test in this bike & almost all the results point out this 250 cc engine isn't a great performer when it comes to fuel economy. It gives you best at 32 km per litre even that when you try to maximize your distance travelled.
The reasons which I have found be like, its two valve engine instead of 4, absence of sixth gear & usual Bazaz engines which are plagued with the low fuel economy in its 250 & 400 cc engines. 
Fz25 & Gixxer will give better mileage than this, I am hundred percent sure about that.
Bazaz is a very good company since it offers value for money bikes specifically when you compare power to performance ratio with others but some obvious downgrades must be highlighted.

Ntorq is one of the best scooter then other scooter
For starting 2. 3 years no doubt ntorq is the best scooty in 125c. Segment. But but but after 3. Years engine and maintenance cost will increase and mileage will decrease  . We have to see around 5 years performance not for 2 ,3 years 
If you have enough money and just wanted to run around 30 to 40k its ntorq always best for long term about 60k rayzr is best 
Engine durability ntorq 6 to 8 years expected 
Rayzr 8 to 12 years expected 

Overall,  if you're looking for a short period 
Go for a ntorq best pickup and good looking good throttle wide trye.  For 3 years no major issues 

If your looking for long term and second hand scooter go for a rayzr long and durability plus Good mileage . You can get  A 50 + millage depending on riding  style

Suggestion for those who are thinking of buying ntorq

Advantage 
1 : looks dami xa
2 : performance ni dami xa 
3 : sound dami xa 
4 : pick up 

Disadvantage 
1 : petrol chai pani piye jasto peunxa
2 : milage chai mero ma 26 dixa aile samma believe gara ki na gara petrol chai lastai khanxa 
3 : suru suru ma dami hunxa scooter tara paxi parts haru ma problem aaunxa mero ma chai meter ma problem aathyo kineko 1 week ma not so good parts haru chai hallinxa off road jada halka paxi paxi 
4 : servicing cost ni high service garna jada pani service center ma no facilities just sabai jana rude hunxan mero ma chai 
5 : fuel indicator ley kati fuel xa vanera dekhaudaina kaile kai 
6 : Fi engine vaye pani starting ma problem aaunxa halka paxi

i ve travelled 11k plus on ride with this one.. i think its not underpowered, torque is good.. light is better than that of xr150, engine is way refined, wealthy looks and the most premium aspect is its monoshock chained suspension.. travelled upto 3500 m height on various bumpy, muddy n stone based roads.. its too much comfort based for both rider and pillion. carried bagpack of abt 10kg on its back on such road, still comfortness didnt decline.
however, required a mudguard on the front wheel base. quick pickup, low n midrange performance is great n comparably defined than others of its segment, still, top end needs to be tuned.
Value for money n comfort..
loved it honestly.

- power

Cons:

- low quality of parts

there are 3 types:

1 : wide angle (less bokeh) fast lenses F1.4 or faster that is in the video 
2 : OIS (optical image stablisation) - this let you hold the camera 1 second (maybe more) instead of 1/25 
3 : software image stabilisation (the dlsr probaly dont have this but could have if they make firmware upgrades)

"4:" tripod
 
smartphones are using ois or equal and multiple pictures
